/**
 * APPLE MUSIC ANDROID 7.0.2 LIQUID GLASS DESIGN SYSTEM
 * - Fundo absoluto OLED (#000000)
 * - Cores de acento adaptativas extraídas da capa tocando (ou Apple Blue #0A84FF)
 * - Liquid Glass Neutro: grafite neutro (#121214) com refração esférica sutil de 10dp nas bordas,
 *   borda fina de 1dp com especular, miolo 100% nítido, blur curto (2..10dp) e zero saturação neon.
 * - Fundo Mesh Animado (Now Playing) misturando 4 cores profundas da capa com rotação lenta.
 */

// ───────────────────────────── Tokens Editorial Dark OLED ─────────────────────────────

object AuroMotion {
    val Emphasized = CubicBezierEasing(0.2f, 0f, 0f, 1f)   // Suave e responsivo
    const val ColorMs = 700                                 // Transição de cores da capa
    const val DriftMs = 18000                               // Onda de fundo do Now Playing
    fun <T> accent() = tween<T>(ColorMs, easing = Emphasized)
    fun <T> liquid() = spring<T>(dampingRatio = 0.82f, stiffness = 280f)
    fun <T> press() = spring<T>(dampingRatio = 0.75f, stiffness = 500f)
}

// ─────────────────────── Cor da capa -> Glow & Mesh ───────────────────────

@Immutable
data class AuroAccent(
    val a: Color,
    val b: Color,
    val mesh1: Color = Color(0xFF3D1214),
    val mesh2: Color = Color(0xFF160D18),
    val mesh3: Color = Color(0xFF2A1015),
    val mesh4: Color = Color(0xFF120A14)
) {
    companion object {
        val WarmRed = Color(0xFF9A1D1D)
        val AppleBlue = Color(0xFF0A84FF)
        val Neutral = AuroAccent(
            a = AppleBlue,
            b = Color(0xFF0071E3),
            mesh1 = Color(0xFF3D1214),
            mesh2 = Color(0xFF160D18),
            mesh3 = Color(0xFF2A1015),
            mesh4 = Color(0xFF120A14)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Normalização e cálculo de cores adaptativas baseadas na capa do álbum (HSL).
 * Garante contraste ótimo com #000000 e #1C1C1E.
 */
object GlowTone {
    fun fit(argb: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
        val r = ((argb shr 16) and 0xFF) / 255f
        val g = ((argb shr 8) and 0xFF) / 255f
        val b = (argb and 0xFF) / 255f
        val hsl = rgbToHsl(r, g, b)
        val h = hsl[0]; val s = hsl[1]; val l = hsl[2]

        return if (s < 0.10f) {
            // Capa monocromática/acromática: Fallback para Azul Apple Music
            0xFF0A84FF.toInt() to 0xFF0071E3.toInt()
        } else {
            // Normaliza S em [0.55, 0.90] e L em [0.56, 0.68] para alto contraste sobre OLED
            val s1 = s.coerceIn(0.55f, 0.90f)
            val l1 = l.coerceIn(0.56f, 0.68f)
            val h2 = (h + 18f / 360f) % 1f
            hslToArgb(h, s1, l1) to hslToArgb(h2, (s1 - 0.05f).coerceIn(0f, 1f), (l1 + 0.04f).coerceIn(0f, 0.72f))
        }
    }

    fun fitMeshColor(argb: Int): Color {
        val r = ((argb shr 16) and 0xFF) / 255f
        val g = ((argb shr 8) and 0xFF) / 255f
        val b = (argb and 0xFF) / 255f
        val hsl = rgbToHsl(r, g, b)
        val h = hsl[0]
        val s = hsl[1].coerceIn(0.35f, 0.78f)
        val l = hsl[2].coerceIn(0.10f, 0.28f) // Tons escuros/atmosféricos para ambient bleed de 2 cores
        return Color(hslToArgb(h, s, l))
    }

    fun rgbToHsl(r: Float, g: Float, b: Float): FloatArray {
        val mx = maxOf(r, g, b); val mn = minOf(r, g, b)
        val l = (mx + mn) / 2f
        if (mx == mn) return floatArrayOf(0f, 0f, l)
        val d = mx - mn
        val s = if (l > 0.5f) d / (2f - mx - mn) else d / (mx + mn)
        val h = when (mx) {
            r -> ((g - b) / d + (if (g < b) 6f else 0f)) / 6f
            g -> ((b - r) / d + 2f) / 6f
            else -> ((r - g) / d + 4f) / 6f
        }
        return floatArrayOf(h, s, l)
    }

    private fun hue(p: Float, q: Float, tIn: Float): Float {
        var t = tIn
        if (t < 0f) t += 1f
        if (t > 1f) t -= 1f
        return when {
            t < 1f / 6f -> p + (q - p) * 6f * t
            t < 1f / 2f -> q
            t < 2f / 3f -> p + (q - p) * (2f / 3f - t) * 6f
            else -> p
        }
    }

    fun hslToArgb(h: Float, s: Float, l: Float): Int {
        val r: Float; val g: Float; val b: Float
        if (s == 0f) { r = l; g = l; b = l } else {
            val q = if (l < 0.5f) l * (1f + s) else l + s - l * s
            val p = 2f * l - q
            r = hue(p, q, h + 1f / 3f); g = hue(p, q, h); b = hue(p, q, h - 1f / 3f)
        }
        fun c(v: Float) = (v * 255f + 0.5f).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)
        return (0xFF shl 24) or (c(r) shl 16) or (c(g) shl 8) or c(b)
    }
}

object CoverGlow {
    private val memoryCache = mutableMapOf<Int, AuroAccent>()

    suspend fun from(cover: Bitmap?, accentColorMode: Int = 0): AuroAccent = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
        if (cover == null) return@withContext AuroAccent.Neutral

        val cacheKey = cover.hashCode() * 31 + accentColorMode
        memoryCache[cacheKey]?.let { return@withContext it }

        try {
            val swBitmap = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O && cover.config == Bitmap.Config.HARDWARE) {
                cover.copy(Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888, false) ?: return@withContext AuroAccent.Neutral
            } else {
                cover
            }

            val p = Palette.from(swBitmap).maximumColorCount(24).resizeBitmapArea(96 * 96).generate()
            val swatches = listOfNotNull(p.vibrantSwatch, p.lightVibrantSwatch, p.darkVibrantSwatch, p.dominantSwatch, p.mutedSwatch)
            val seed = swatches.maxByOrNull { it.population * (0.25f + it.hsl[1]) }

            val (aInt, bInt) = if (seed != null) {
                GlowTone.fit(seed.rgb)
            } else {
                0xFF9A1D1D.toInt() to 0xFF3D1214.toInt()
            }

            // 4 cores para o mesh animado do Now Playing (fallback neutro quente escuro #3D1214 e #160D18)
            val m1 = GlowTone.fitMeshColor(p.vibrantSwatch?.rgb ?: seed?.rgb ?: 0xFF3D1214.toInt())
            val m2 = GlowTone.fitMeshColor(p.darkVibrantSwatch?.rgb ?: p.dominantSwatch?.rgb ?: 0xFF160D18.toInt())
            val m3 = GlowTone.fitMeshColor(p.lightVibrantSwatch?.rgb ?: p.mutedSwatch?.rgb ?: 0xFF2A1015.toInt())
            val m4 = GlowTone.fitMeshColor(seed?.rgb ?: 0xFF120A14.toInt())

            val finalAccent = if (accentColorMode == 1) {
                // Modo fixo Azul Apple
                AuroAccent(
                    a = AuroAccent.AppleBlue,
                    b = Color(0xFF0071E3),
                    mesh1 = m1,
                    mesh2 = m2,
                    mesh3 = m3,
                    mesh4 = m4
                )
            } else {
                AuroAccent(
                    a = Color(aInt),
                    b = Color(bInt),
                    mesh1 = m1,
                    mesh2 = m2,
                    mesh3 = m3,
                    mesh4 = m4
                )
            }

            if (memoryCache.size > 100) memoryCache.clear()
            memoryCache[cacheKey] = finalAccent
            finalAccent
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            AuroAccent.Neutral
        }
    }
}

// ─────────────────── Luz do ouvinte: inclinação do celular ───────────────────

// <AuroTilt>
/** Inclinação do celular -> direção da luz do ouvinte. Pura (sem Android), testável na JVM. */
object AuroTilt {
    fun light(gx: Float, gy: Float, sideGain: Float = 1.6f, bias: Float = 0.25f): FloatArray {
        val x = (gx / 9.81f) * sideGain
        val y = -(gy / 9.81f) - bias
        val len = sqrt(x * x + y * y)
        val inPlane = sqrt(gx * gx + gy * gy) / 9.81f
        val gain = 0.6f + 0.4f * inPlane.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        return if (len < 1e-4f) floatArrayOf(0f, -1f, gain) else floatArrayOf(x / len, y / len, gain)
    }
}

class TiltSmoother(private val omega: Float = 7f, private val zeta: Float = 0.8f) {
    var x = 0f
        private set
    var y = 1f
        private set
    var gain = 1f
        private set
    private var vx = 0f
    private var vy = 0f
    private var vg = 0f
    private var last = 0L

    fun step(tx: Float, ty: Float, tg: Float, nanos: Long) {
        val dt = if (last == 0L) 0.02f else ((nanos - last) / 1e9f).coerceIn(0.001f, 0.05f)
        last = nanos
        vx += (omega * omega * (tx - x) - 2f * zeta * omega * vx) * dt
        x += vx * dt
        vy += (omega * omega * (ty - y) - 2f * zeta * omega * vy) * dt
        y += vy * dt
        vg += (omega * omega * (tg - gain) - 2f * zeta * omega * vg) * dt
        gain += vg * dt
    }
}
// </AuroTilt>

// ─────────────────── CompositionLocals & Tema ───────────────────

@Immutable
data class AuroLight(val dx: Float = 0f, val dy: Float = 1f, val gain: Float = 1f)

class AuroAccentState(
    val a: State<Color>,
    val b: State<Color>,
    val mesh1: State<Color>,
    val mesh2: State<Color>,
    val mesh3: State<Color>,
    val mesh4: State<Color>
)

val LocalAuroLight = staticCompositionLocalOf<() -> AuroLight> { { AuroLight() } }
val LocalAuroTime = staticCompositionLocalOf<() -> Float> { { 0f } }
val LocalAuroAccent = staticCompositionLocalOf<AuroAccentState> {
    AuroAccentState(
        object : State<Color> { override val value = AuroAccent.Neutral.a },
        object : State<Color> { override val value = AuroAccent.Neutral.b },
        object : State<Color> { override val value = AuroAccent.Neutral.mesh1 },
        object : State<Color> { override val value = AuroAccent.Neutral.mesh2 },
        object : State<Color> { override val value = AuroAccent.Neutral.mesh3 },
        object : State<Color> { override val value = AuroAccent.Neutral.mesh4 }
    )
}

val LocalAuroSoft = staticCompositionLocalOf { 0.6f }
val LocalReduceMotionOverride = staticCompositionLocalOf<Boolean?> { null }
val LocalReduceTransparency = staticCompositionLocalOf { false }
val LocalAccentColorMode = staticCompositionLocalOf { 0 }
val LocalAuroBottomInset = compositionLocalOf { 0.dp }
val LocalAuroIsPlaying = compositionLocalOf { false }

@Composable
fun rememberReduceMotion(): Boolean {
    val override = LocalReduceMotionOverride.current
    if (override != null) return override
    val context = LocalContext.current
    return remember { Settings.Global.getFloat(context.contentResolver, Settings.Global.ANIMATOR_DURATION_SCALE, 1f) == 0f }
}

@Composable
fun rememberListenerLight(playing: Boolean, tilt: Boolean = true): () -> AuroLight {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    val rawTarget = remember { FloatArray(3) { if (it == 1) -1f else if (it == 2) 1f else 0f } }
    val currentLightRef = remember { arrayOf(AuroLight()) }
    var drawTrigger by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }

    DisposableEffect(tilt, lifecycleOwner) {
        val sm = context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as SensorManager
        val sensor = sm.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_GRAVITY) ?: sm.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER)
        if (!tilt || sensor == null) {
            currentLightRef[0] = AuroLight()
            return@DisposableEffect onDispose {}
        }
        val listener = object : SensorEventListener {
            override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
                val l = AuroTilt.light(event.values[0], event.values[1])
                rawTarget[0] = l[0]
                rawTarget[1] = l[1]
                rawTarget[2] = l[2]
            }

            override fun onAccuracyChanged(s: Sensor?, accuracy: Int) {}
        }

        var isRegistered = false
        fun register() {
            if (!isRegistered) {
                sm.registerListener(listener, sensor, SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_GAME)
                isRegistered = true
            }
        }
        fun unregister() {
            if (isRegistered) {
                sm.unregisterListener(listener)
                isRegistered = false
            }
        }

        if (lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.STARTED)) {
            register()
        }

        val observer = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
            when (event) {
                Lifecycle.Event.ON_START -> register()
                Lifecycle.Event.ON_STOP -> unregister()
                else -> Unit
            }
        }
        lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)

        onDispose {
            lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer)
            unregister()
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(tilt, lifecycleOwner) {
        if (!tilt) return@LaunchedEffect
        val smoother = TiltSmoother()
        while (isActive) {
            withFrameNanos { nanos ->
                if (lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.STARTED)) {
                    smoother.step(rawTarget[0], rawTarget[1], rawTarget[2], nanos)
                    val len = sqrt(smoother.x * smoother.x + smoother.y * smoother.y).coerceAtLeast(1e-4f)
                    val next = AuroLight(smoother.x / len, smoother.y / len, smoother.gain)
                    val cur = currentLightRef[0]
                    if (abs(next.dx - cur.dx) + abs(next.dy - cur.dy) + abs(next.gain - cur.gain) > 0.0005f) {
                        currentLightRef[0] = next
                        drawTrigger++
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    val transition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "light")
    val phase = transition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0f,
        targetValue = 1f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(tween(AuroMotion.DriftMs, easing = LinearEasing)),
        label = "phase",
    )
    val amount = animateFloatAsState(if (playing && tilt) 1f else 0f, AuroMotion.liquid(), label = "breath")
    return {
        @Suppress("UNUSED_VARIABLE")
        val v = drawTrigger
        val s = currentLightRef[0]
        val th = 0.07f * amount.value * sin((phase.value * 2f * PI).toFloat())
        val c = cos(th)
        val sn = sin(th)
        AuroLight(dx = s.dx * c - s.dy * sn, dy = s.dx * sn + s.dy * c, gain = s.gain)
    }
}

@Composable
fun rememberAuroClock(running: Boolean): () -> Float {
    val t = produceState(0f, running) {
        if (!running) return@produceState
        val start = withFrameNanos { it }
        val offset = value
        while (true) withFrameNanos { value = offset + (it - start) / 1_000_000_000f }
    }
    return { t.value }
}

@Composable
fun AuroTheme(
    accent: AuroAccent,
    playing: Boolean = true,
    tilt: Boolean = true,
    soft: Float = 0.6f,
    reduceTransparency: Boolean = false,
    accentColorMode: Int = 0,
    reduceMotionOverride: Boolean? = null,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val reduce = if (reduceMotionOverride != null) reduceMotionOverride else rememberReduceMotion()
    val a = animateColorAsState(accent.a, if (reduce) tween(0) else AuroMotion.accent(), label = "accentA")
    val b = animateColorAsState(accent.b, if (reduce) tween(0) else AuroMotion.accent(), label = "accentB")
    val m1 = animateColorAsState(accent.mesh1, if (reduce) tween(0) else AuroMotion.accent(), label = "mesh1")
    val m2 = animateColorAsState(accent.mesh2, if (reduce) tween(0) else AuroMotion.accent(), label = "mesh2")
    val m3 = animateColorAsState(accent.mesh3, if (reduce) tween(0) else AuroMotion.accent(), label = "mesh3")
    val m4 = animateColorAsState(accent.mesh4, if (reduce) tween(0) else AuroMotion.accent(), label = "mesh4")

    val state = remember(a, b, m1, m2, m3, m4) { AuroAccentState(a, b, m1, m2, m3, m4) }
    val light = rememberListenerLight(playing && !reduce, tilt && !reduce)
    val clock = rememberAuroClock(playing && !reduce)

    CompositionLocalProvider(
        LocalAuroAccent provides state,
        LocalAuroLight provides light,
        LocalAuroTime provides clock,
        LocalAuroSoft provides soft,
        LocalReduceTransparency provides reduceTransparency,
        LocalAccentColorMode provides accentColorMode,
        LocalReduceMotionOverride provides reduceMotionOverride
    ) {
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(AuroColor.Black)
        ) {
            content()
        }
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────── Superfícies Editorial OLED ───────────────────────────

enum class AuroGlassKind { Button, Card }

/**
 * Superfície limpa do design Editorial Dark OLED:
 * - Fundo fosco translúcido (#18181B @ 82%) ou branco translúcido (#FFFFFF @ 22% para Button)
 * - Borda fina e precisa de 0.5.dp a 10% branco
 * - Zero blur/refração pesada, garantindo 120Hz constantes e leveza total
 * - Animação de toque com escala 0.95f e mola spring(0.75f, 500f)
 */
@Composable
fun LiquidGlassSurface(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    shape: Shape = CircleShape,
    soft: Float = LocalAuroSoft.current,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    haptic: ((View) -> Unit)? = { AuroHaptics.click(it) },
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    val interaction = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val pressed by interaction.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val pressScale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (pressed) 0.95f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.75f, stiffness = 500f),
        label = "glassPressScale"
    )

    val clickModifier = if (onClick != null) {
        Modifier.clickable(interactionSource = interaction, indication = null) {
            haptic?.invoke(view)
            onClick()
        }
    } else Modifier

    val isButton = shape == CircleShape
    val surfaceColor = if (isButton) AuroColor.ControlGlass else Color(0xD118181B)

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .graphicsLayer {
                scaleX = pressScale
                scaleY = pressScale
            }
            .clip(shape)
            .background(surfaceColor, shape)
            .border(0.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f), shape)
            .then(clickModifier),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
        content = content
    )
}

/**
 * Componente unificado AuroGlass: mapeia diretamente para LiquidGlassSurface
 * mantendo compatibilidade total com as chamadas existentes.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroGlass(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    kind: AuroGlassKind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    haptic: ((View) -> Unit)? = { AuroHaptics.click(it) },
    soft: Float = LocalAuroSoft.current,
    cornerRadius: Dp? = null,
    specMultiplier: Float = 1f,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val isButton = kind == AuroGlassKind.Button
    val shape = if (isButton) CircleShape else RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius ?: AuroRadius.card)

    LiquidGlassSurface(
        modifier = modifier,
        shape = shape,
        soft = soft,
        onClick = onClick,
        haptic = haptic,
        content = content
    )
}

/**
 * Fundo Borrado Animado de 2 Cores do Álbum (Now Playing + Letras):
 * - Extrai 2 cores reais da capa atual (mesh1 e mesh2 do CoverGlow)
 * - 2 manchas radiais amplas e super difusas em órbita orgânica (16s)
 * - Base #050505, 120Hz constante, zero BlurEffect recursivo
 */
@Composable
fun AnimatedDualColorBackground(
    color1: Color,
    color2: Color,
    isPlaying: Boolean = true,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit = {}
) {
    val reduceMotion = rememberReduceMotion()

    val animColor1 by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = color1,
        animationSpec = tween(AuroMotion.ColorMs, easing = AuroMotion.Emphasized),
        label = "dualBgColor1"
    )
    val animColor2 by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = color2,
        animationSpec = tween(AuroMotion.ColorMs, easing = AuroMotion.Emphasized),
        label = "dualBgColor2"
    )

    val transition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "dualColorOrbit")
    val orbitPhase by transition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0f,
        targetValue = 1f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(16000, easing = LinearEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Restart
        ),
        label = "orbitPhase"
    )

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xFF050505))
            .drawBehind {
                val t = if (isPlaying && !reduceMotion) (orbitPhase * 2f * PI).toFloat() else 0f
                val w = size.width
                val h = size.height

                // Mancha 1: órbita suave no quadrante superior/central
                val c1X = w * (0.45f + 0.22f * cos(t))
                val c1Y = h * (0.32f + 0.14f * sin(t))
                val r1 = max(w, h) * 0.78f

                drawRect(
                    brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            animColor1.copy(alpha = 0.58f),
                            animColor1.copy(alpha = 0.22f),
                            Color.Transparent
                        ),
                        center = Offset(c1X, c1Y),
                        radius = r1
                    )
                )

                // Mancha 2: órbita suave no quadrante médio/inferior em fase oposta
                val c2X = w * (0.55f + 0.22f * cos(-t + 2.4f))
                val c2Y = h * (0.58f + 0.16f * sin(-t + 2.4f))
                val r2 = max(w, h) * 0.82f

                drawRect(
                    brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            animColor2.copy(alpha = 0.52f),
                            animColor2.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                            Color.Transparent
                        ),
                        center = Offset(c2X, c2Y),
                        radius = r2
                    )
                )
            },
        content = content
    )
}

/**
 * Fundo com Ambient Bleed suave extraído da capa:
 * Base #050505 com gradiente vertical suave da cor da capa desvanecendo para transparente.
 * Zero shader pesado, garantindo 120Hz perfeitos.
 */
@Composable
fun AmbientBleedBackground(
    color: Color,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val animatedColor by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = color,
        animationSpec = tween(AuroMotion.ColorMs, easing = AuroMotion.Emphasized),
        label = "ambientBleedColor"
    )

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Canvas)
            .drawBehind {
                drawRect(
                    brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            animatedColor.copy(alpha = 0.50f),
                            animatedColor.copy(alpha = 0.15f),
                            Color.Transparent
                        ),
                        startY = 0f,
                        endY = size.height * 0.70f
                    )
                )
            },
        content = content
    )
}

// ─────────────────────── Fundo Mesh Animado (Now Playing) ───────────────────────

/**
 * Fundo animado de gradientes fluidos estilo Apple Music Android 7.0.2 (LyricsBackgroundLayerView).
 * Mistura 4 cores escuras/profundas extraídas da capa com rotação lenta, controlado por LocalAuroTime.
 * Pausa em ON_STOP ou quando reduceMotion estiver ativo (0 custo de GPU quando parado).
 */
@Composable
fun LiquidMeshBackground(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isPlaying: Boolean = true,
    content: @Composable BoxScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val clock = LocalAuroTime.current
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current
    val reduceMotion = rememberReduceMotion()

    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU && !reduceMotion) {
        val shader = remember { RuntimeShader(LIQUID_MESH_AGSL) }
        val brush = remember(shader) { ShaderBrush(shader) }

        Box(
            modifier = modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .drawBehind {
                    val time = if (isPlaying) clock() else 0f
                    val c1 = accent.mesh1.value
                    val c2 = accent.mesh2.value
                    val c3 = accent.mesh3.value
                    val c4 = accent.mesh4.value
                    shader.setFloatUniform("uSize", size.width, size.height)
                    shader.setFloatUniform("uTime", time)
                    shader.setFloatUniform("uColor1", c1.red, c1.green, c1.blue, 1f)
                    shader.setFloatUniform("uColor2", c2.red, c2.green, c2.blue, 1f)
                    shader.setFloatUniform("uColor3", c3.red, c3.green, c3.blue, 1f)
                    shader.setFloatUniform("uColor4", c4.red, c4.green, c4.blue, 1f)
                    drawRect(brush)
                },
            content = content
        )
    } else {
        // Fallback: gradiente estático profundo
        val c1 = accent.mesh1.value
        val c2 = accent.mesh2.value
        val c4 = accent.mesh4.value

        Box(
            modifier = modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            c1.copy(alpha = 0.85f),
                            c2.copy(alpha = 0.90f),
                            c4.copy(alpha = 0.95f),
                            AuroColor.Black
                        )
                    )
                ),
            content = content
        )
    }
}

// ─────────────────────── AGSL Shaders Nativos (API 33+) ───────────────────────

/**
 * Shader AGSL do Liquid Glass: grafite neutro (#121214) com refração nas bordas externas
 * de ~10dp, especular sutil na borda superior e centro nítido.
 */
private const val IOS_LIQUID_GLASS_AGSL = """
uniform float2 uSize;
uniform float  uRadius;
uniform float  uSoft;
uniform float2 uLightDir;
uniform float3 uAccent;
uniform float  uPx;
uniform float  uTintAlpha;

float sdRound(float2 p, float2 halfSize, float radius) {
    float2 q = abs(p) - halfSize + radius;
    return min(max(q.x, q.y), 0.0) + length(max(q, 0.0)) - radius;
}

float2 getNormal(float2 p, float2 halfSize, float radius) {
    float eps = 1.0;
    float dx = sdRound(p + float2(eps, 0.0), halfSize, radius) - sdRound(p - float2(eps, 0.0), halfSize, radius);
    float dy = sdRound(p + float2(0.0, eps), halfSize, radius) - sdRound(p - float2(0.0, eps), halfSize, radius);
    float2 n = float2(dx, dy);
    float l = length(n);
    return (l > 0.0001) ? (n / l) : float2(0.0, 1.0);
}

half4 main(float2 fragCoord) {
    float2 halfSize = 0.5 * uSize;
    float2 p = fragCoord - halfSize;
    float radius = clamp(uRadius, 0.0, min(halfSize.x, halfSize.y));
    
    float d = sdRound(p, halfSize, radius);
    
    // Borda externa de ~10dp para refração e anel especular
    float rimWidth = 10.0 * uPx;
    // Dentro da forma, d <= 0.0, a distância até a borda externa é -d
    float distToRim = -d;
    float rimFactor = 1.0 - clamp(distToRim / rimWidth, 0.0, 1.0);

    // Reflexo especular sutil na direção da luz do ouvinte ao longo da normal da borda
    float2 n = getNormal(p, halfSize, radius);
    float spec = max(dot(n, -uLightDir), 0.0);
    spec = pow(spec, 4.0) * rimFactor * 0.35;

    // Grafite neutro #121214
    float3 graphite = float3(0.070, 0.070, 0.078);
    float3 col = graphite + spec * (float3(1.0) * 0.8 + uAccent * 0.2);

    float alpha = clamp(uTintAlpha + rimFactor * 0.15, 0.0, 0.95);
    return half4(half3(col * alpha), half(alpha));
}
"""

/**
 * Shader AGSL do Mesh Animado de Fundo (Now Playing):
 * Três vórtices lentos inspirados no algoritmo LyricsBackgroundLayerView do Apple Music.
 */
private const val LIQUID_MESH_AGSL = """
uniform float2 uSize;
uniform float  uTime;
uniform float4 uColor1;
uniform float4 uColor2;
uniform float4 uColor3;
uniform float4 uColor4;

half4 main(float2 fragCoord) {
    float2 uv = fragCoord / uSize;
    float t = uTime * 0.08;

    // Três centros orgânicos em rotação lenta
    float2 c1 = float2(0.50 + 0.35 * cos(t * 0.8), 0.35 + 0.25 * sin(t * 0.8));
    float2 c2 = float2(0.50 + 0.38 * cos(-t * 0.6 + 2.09), 0.65 + 0.25 * sin(-t * 0.6 + 2.09));
    float2 c3 = float2(0.50 + 0.30 * sin(t * 0.5 + 4.18), 0.50 + 0.28 * cos(t * 0.5 + 4.18));

    float d1 = smoothstep(0.95, 0.05, length(uv - c1));
    float d2 = smoothstep(0.95, 0.05, length(uv - c2));
    float d3 = smoothstep(0.95, 0.05, length(uv - c3));

    // Mistura aditiva/interpolada
    float3 col = uColor1.rgb * (1.0 - uv.y * 0.25);
    col = mix(col, uColor2.rgb, clamp(d1, 0.0, 1.0));
    col = mix(col, uColor3.rgb, clamp(d2 * 0.85, 0.0, 1.0));
    col = mix(col, uColor4.rgb, clamp(d3 * 0.75, 0.0, 1.0));

    // Scrims Apple Music: vinheta radial e escurecimento do rodapé para contraste total
    float vig = smoothstep(1.3, 0.3, length((uv - float2(0.5, 0.5)) * float2(1.0, 1.3)));
    col *= (0.60 + 0.40 * vig);

    float bottomScrim = smoothstep(1.0, 0.70, uv.y);
    col *= (0.70 + 0.30 * bottomScrim);

    return half4(half3(col), 1.0);
}
"""

=== FILE: app/src/main/java/com/lucas/player/ui/components/AuroComponents.kt ===

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - COMPONENTES
 * Todos os objetos de vidro leem a luz global e são fundidos sobre o preto absoluto (#000000).
 */

fun formatTimeMs(ms: Long): String {
    val totalSec = (ms / 1000).coerceAtLeast(0)
    val hours = totalSec / 3600
    val min = (totalSec % 3600) / 60
    val sec = totalSec % 60
    return if (hours > 0) {
        String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%d:%02d:%02d", hours, min, sec)
    } else {
        String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%02d:%02d", min, sec)
    }
}

@Composable
fun rememberTrackCover(track: Track?, size: Int = 300): Bitmap? {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var bitmap by remember(track?.id, size) {
        mutableStateOf(track?.let { CoverArtLoader.getCachedCover(it, size) })
    }
    LaunchedEffect(track?.id, size) {
        if (track != null) {
            if (bitmap == null) {
                CoverArtLoader.loadCover(context, track, size) { bmp ->
                    bitmap = bmp
                }
            }
        } else {
            bitmap = null
        }
    }
    return bitmap
}

/**
 * 1. BARRA DE PROGRESSO OFICIAL DO AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM
 * Spec: "trilho 3dp #2D2D2D, preenchimento Accent A, bolinha branca de 10dp. Tempos em Label/TextSecondary."
 */
@Composable
fun AuroProgressBar(
    progressFraction: Float,
    durationMs: Long,
    onSeek: (Float) -> Unit,
    onSeekJumpBackward: () -> Unit = {},
    onSeekJumpForward: () -> Unit = {},
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    var isDragging by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var dragFraction by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }
    var showRemainingTime by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

    val activeFraction = (if (isDragging) dragFraction else progressFraction).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    val displayedMs = (activeFraction * durationMs).toLong()
    val remainingMs = (durationMs - displayedMs).coerceAtLeast(0)

    val seekBackLabel = stringResource(R.string.cd_seek_back_10)
    val seekForwardLabel = stringResource(R.string.cd_seek_forward_10)
    val cdProgress = stringResource(R.string.cd_track_progress)
    val stateDesc = stringResource(R.string.progress_state_desc, formatTimeMs(displayedMs), formatTimeMs(durationMs))

    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        // Trilho de 3dp com thumb de 10dp
        BoxWithConstraints(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(48.dp) // Aumentado de 30.dp para 48.dp (trilho interno continua 3.dp)
                .semantics(mergeDescendants = true) {
                    contentDescription = cdProgress
                    stateDescription = stateDesc
                    progressBarRangeInfo = ProgressBarRangeInfo(
                        current = displayedMs.toFloat(),
                        range = 0f..(durationMs.toFloat().coerceAtLeast(1f)),
                        steps = 0
                    )
                    setProgress { targetValue ->
                        if (durationMs > 0) {
                            val targetFrac = (targetValue / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            onSeek(targetFrac)
                            true
                        } else false
                    }
                    customActions = listOf(
                        CustomAccessibilityAction(seekBackLabel) {
                            if (durationMs > 0) {
                                val currentMs = (activeFraction * durationMs).toLong()
                                val newMs = (currentMs - 10_000L).coerceAtLeast(0L)
                                onSeek(newMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat())
                                onSeekJumpBackward()
                                true
                            } else false
                        },
                        CustomAccessibilityAction(seekForwardLabel) {
                            if (durationMs > 0) {
                                val currentMs = (activeFraction * durationMs).toLong()
                                val newMs = (currentMs + 10_000L).coerceAtMost(durationMs)
                                onSeek(newMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat())
                                onSeekJumpForward()
                                true
                            } else false
                        }
                    )
                }
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectDragGestures(
                        onDragStart = { offset ->
                            isDragging = true
                            dragFraction = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            AuroHaptics.click(view)
                        },
                        onDragEnd = {
                            onSeek(dragFraction)
                            isDragging = false
                            AuroHaptics.confirm(view)
                        },
                        onDragCancel = {
                            isDragging = false
                        }
                    ) { change, _ ->
                        change.consume()
                        val newFrac = (change.position.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        if ((newFrac * 20).toInt() != (dragFraction * 20).toInt()) {
                            AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                        }
                        dragFraction = newFrac
                    }
                }
                .pointerInput(Unit) {
                    detectTapGestures { offset ->
                        val frac = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        AuroHaptics.click(view)
                        onSeek(frac)
                    }
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
        ) {
            val totalWidth = maxWidth
            val thumbOffset = ((totalWidth - 10.dp) * activeFraction).coerceAtLeast(0.dp)

            // Trilho #2D2D2D
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(3.dp)
                    .background(AuroColor.Track, RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
            )

            // Preenchimento com Branco Puro
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(activeFraction)
                    .height(3.dp)
                    .background(Color.White, RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
            )

            // Bolinha branca de 10dp
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .offset(x = thumbOffset)
                    .size(10.dp)
                    .background(Color.White, CircleShape)
            )
        }

        // Tempos (ex: 1:24 e -3:41 conforme o espécime)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 2.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = formatTimeMs(displayedMs),
                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                    fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                )
            )
            Text(
                text = if (showRemainingTime) "-${formatTimeMs(remainingMs)}" else formatTimeMs(durationMs),
                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                    fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                ),
                modifier = Modifier.clickable { showRemainingTime = !showRemainingTime }
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * EDITORIAL FLOATING NAVBAR (Editorial Dark OLED)
 * Pílula flutuante (altura 56dp, CircleShape, margem lateral 24dp).
 * 4 abas minimalistas com apenas ícones: Início, Buscar, Biblioteca, Ajustes/Áudio.
 * Fundo #161618 @ 88% + borda 0.5dp White 8%.
 */
@Composable
fun EditorialFloatingNavbar(
    currentScreen: NavScreen,
    onHomeClick: () -> Unit,
    onSearchClick: () -> Unit,
    onLibraryClick: () -> Unit,
    onTuneClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp)
            .height(56.dp)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(AuroColor.NavBarBg)
            .border(0.5.dp, AuroColor.NavBarBorder, CircleShape)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceAround,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            EditorialNavIcon(
                activeIcon = Icons.Rounded.Home,
                inactiveIcon = Icons.Outlined.Home,
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.tab_home),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Home,
                onClick = onHomeClick
            )
            EditorialNavIcon(
                activeIcon = Icons.Rounded.Search,
                inactiveIcon = Icons.Outlined.Search,
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.tab_search),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Search,
                onClick = onSearchClick
            )
            EditorialNavIcon(
                activeIcon = Icons.Rounded.Favorite,
                inactiveIcon = Icons.Outlined.FavoriteBorder,
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.tab_library),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.LibraryRoot || currentScreen is NavScreen.CategoryList || currentScreen is NavScreen.Drilldown,
                onClick = onLibraryClick
            )
            EditorialNavIcon(
                activeIcon = Icons.Rounded.Tune,
                inactiveIcon = Icons.Outlined.Tune,
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.tab_tune),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Equalizer || currentScreen is NavScreen.Settings,
                onClick = onTuneClick
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun EditorialNavIcon(
    activeIcon: ImageVector,
    inactiveIcon: ImageVector,
    contentDescription: String,
    isActive: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isPressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.88f else if (isActive) 1.05f else 1f,
        animationSpec = AuroMotion.press(),
        label = "nav_icon_scale"
    )

    val tint by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = if (isActive) Color.White else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.45f),
        animationSpec = tween(200),
        label = "nav_icon_tint"
    )

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .size(48.dp)
            .scale(scale)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.tick(view)
                    onClick()
                }
            ),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = if (isActive) activeIcon else inactiveIcon,
            contentDescription = contentDescription,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
        )
    }
}

 * APPLE FLOATING TAB BAR - Retrocompatibilidade com EditorialFloatingNavbar

@Composable
fun AppleFloatingTabBar(

        onSearchClick = onSearchClick,

        modifier = modifier
    )
}

/**
 * TRANSLUCENT PLAY BUTTON (Editorial Dark OLED)
 * Botão circular translúcido (#FFFFFF @ 22%) com ícone branco e escala interativa.
 */
@Composable
fun TranslucentPlayButton(
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    size: Dp = 40.dp,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isPressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.92f else 1f,
        animationSpec = AuroMotion.press(),
        label = "translucent_play_scale"
    )

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .size(size)
            .scale(scale)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(AuroColor.ControlGlass)
            .border(0.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f), CircleShape)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                    onClick()
                }
            ),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = if (isPlaying) Icons.Rounded.Pause else Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
            contentDescription = if (isPlaying) stringResource(R.string.cd_pause) else stringResource(R.string.cd_play),
            tint = Color.White,
            modifier = Modifier.size(size * 0.52f)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * EDITORIAL MINI PLAYER (Editorial Dark OLED)
 * Pílula minimalista (altura 52dp, CircleShape, margem lateral 20dp).
 * Capa 36dp (raio 8dp), título 13.5sp, artista 12sp, TranslucentPlayButton (34dp) e SkipNext (20dp).
 */
@Composable
fun EditorialMiniPlayer(
    track: Track?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    progressMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    coverBitmap: Bitmap?,
    onOpenNowPlaying: () -> Unit,
    onPlayPause: () -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
    onPrevious: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    if (track == null) return

    val view = LocalView.current
    val progressFraction = if (durationMs > 0) {
        (progressMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    } else 0f

    var totalDragX by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }
    var totalDragY by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(52.dp)
            .padding(horizontal = 20.dp)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(AuroColor.SurfaceCard)
            .border(0.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), CircleShape)
            .clickable(onClick = onOpenNowPlaying)
    ) {
        // Linha de progresso discreta de 2dp na base
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(2.dp)
                .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp)
                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), CircleShape)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(progressFraction)
                    .fillMaxHeight()
                    .background(Color.White, CircleShape)
            )
        }

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(horizontal = 10.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            // Capa 36dp com raio 8dp
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(36.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(AuroRadius.thumbnail)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (coverBitmap != null) {
                    Image(
                        bitmap = coverBitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_album_cover),
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                    )
                } else {
                    AuroCoverPlaceholder(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        glass = false,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(AuroRadius.thumbnail)
                    )
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))

            // Metadados com suporte a gestos
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .pointerInput(Unit) {
                        detectDragGestures(
                            onDragStart = {
                                totalDragX = 0f
                                totalDragY = 0f
                            },
                            onDragEnd = {
                                if (abs(totalDragY) > abs(totalDragX) && totalDragY < -20f) {
                                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                    onOpenNowPlaying()
                                } else if (abs(totalDragX) > 30f) {
                                    if (totalDragX < 0) {
                                        AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                        onNext()
                                    } else {
                                        AuroHaptics.click(view)
                                        onPrevious()
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        ) { change, dragAmount ->
                            change.consume()
                            totalDragX += dragAmount.x
                            totalDragY += dragAmount.y
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(end = 4.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = track.title,
                    style = AuroType.TrackTitle,
                    color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(1.dp))
                Text(
                    text = track.artist.ifEmpty { stringResource(R.string.unknown_artist) },
                    style = AuroType.Subtitle,
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }

            // Botão Play Translúcido (34dp)
            TranslucentPlayButton(
                isPlaying = isPlaying,
                size = 34.dp,
                onClick = onPlayPause
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))

            // SkipNext (20dp)
            IconButton(
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                    onNext()
                },
                modifier = Modifier.size(34.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.SkipNext,
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_next),
                    tint = Color.White,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * APPLE MINI PLAYER - Retrocompatibilidade
 */
@Composable
fun AppleMiniPlayer(
    track: Track?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    progressMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    coverBitmap: Bitmap?,
    onOpenNowPlaying: () -> Unit,
    onPlayPause: () -> Unit,

        isPlaying = isPlaying,
        progressMs = progressMs,
        durationMs = durationMs,
        coverBitmap = coverBitmap,
        onOpenNowPlaying = onOpenNowPlaying,
        onPlayPause = onPlayPause,
        onNext = onNext,
        onPrevious = onPrevious,
        modifier = modifier
    )
}

/**
 * EDITORIAL SCRUBBER (Editorial Dark OLED)
 * Barra de progresso com trilho de 2.5dp, thumb de 10dp e números monoespaçados.
 */
@Composable
fun EditorialScrubber(
    progressMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    onSeek: (Long) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    activeColor: Color = AuroColor.ScrubberActive,
    trackColor: Color = AuroColor.ScrubberTrack,
    showTimestampsOnlyWhenDragging: Boolean = true
) {
    var isDragging by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var draggingFraction by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(-1f) }
    val view = LocalView.current
    val currentFraction = if (durationMs > 0) (progressMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f) else 0f
    val displayFraction = if (draggingFraction >= 0f) draggingFraction else currentFraction

    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(28.dp)
                .pointerInput(durationMs) {
                    detectDragGestures(
                        onDragStart = { offset ->
                            isDragging = true
                            val f = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                            draggingFraction = f
                            AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                        },
                        onDragEnd = {
                            if (draggingFraction >= 0f) {
                                onSeek((draggingFraction * durationMs).toLong())
                            }
                            draggingFraction = -1f
                            isDragging = false
                            AuroHaptics.click(view)
                        },
                        onDragCancel = {
                            draggingFraction = -1f
                            isDragging = false
                        }
                    ) { change, _ ->
                        change.consume()
                        val f = (change.position.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        draggingFraction = f
                    }
                }
                .pointerInput(durationMs) {
                    detectTapGestures { offset ->
                        val f = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        onSeek((f * durationMs).toLong())
                        AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                    }
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
        ) {
            // Track base
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(2.5.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(trackColor)
            )
            // Active progress
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(displayFraction)
                    .height(2.5.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(activeColor)
            )
            // Thumb
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(displayFraction),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterEnd
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(10.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(Color.White)
                )
            }
        }

        // Time labels com AnimatedVisibility
        AnimatedVisibility(
            visible = !showTimestampsOnlyWhenDragging || isDragging,
            enter = fadeIn(tween(150)),
            exit = fadeOut(tween(150))
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(start = 2.dp, end = 2.dp, top = 2.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
            ) {
                val elapsed = if (draggingFraction >= 0f) (draggingFraction * durationMs).toLong() else progressMs
                val remaining = (durationMs - elapsed).coerceAtLeast(0)

                Text(
                    text = formatTimeMs(elapsed),
                    style = AuroType.MicroLabel.copy(
                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                        fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                    ),
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary
                )
                Text(
                    text = "-${formatTimeMs(remaining)}",
                    style = AuroType.MicroLabel.copy(
                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                        fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                    ),
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Transição suave de fade no scroll para que o conteúdo passe por trás das barras flutuantes.
 */
@Composable
fun ScrollEdgeFade(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    height: Dp = 32.dp,
    top: Boolean = false
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(height)
            .background(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    colors = if (top) {
                        listOf(AuroColor.Black, Color.Transparent)
                    } else {
                        listOf(Color.Transparent, AuroColor.Black)
                    }
                )
            )
    )
}

/**
 * Controle de Volume estilo Apple Music com ícones nas pontas e trilho de 6dp.
 */
@Composable
fun AppleVolumeSlider(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val audioManager = remember { context.getSystemService(Context.AUDIO_SERVICE) as android.media.AudioManager }
    val maxVol = remember { audioManager.getStreamMaxVolume(android.media.AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC) }
    var currentVol by remember {
        mutableIntStateOf(audioManager.getStreamVolume(android.media.AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC))
    }
    val view = LocalView.current

    // Sincronizar com botões físicos de volume em tempo real
    DisposableEffect(context) {
        val receiver = object : android.content.BroadcastReceiver() {
            override fun onReceive(c: Context?, intent: android.content.Intent?) {
                currentVol = audioManager.getStreamVolume(android.media.AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC)
            }
        }
        val filter = android.content.IntentFilter("android.media.VOLUME_CHANGED_ACTION")
        ContextCompat.registerReceiver(context, receiver, filter, ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED)
        onDispose {
            try {
                context.unregisterReceiver(receiver)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    val fraction = if (maxVol > 0) (currentVol.toFloat() / maxVol.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f) else 0f

    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.VolumeDown,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )

        BoxWithConstraints(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .height(28.dp)
                .pointerInput(maxVol) {
                    detectTapGestures { offset ->
                        val f = (offset.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        val targetVol = (f * maxVol).toInt().coerceIn(0, maxVol)
                        audioManager.setStreamVolume(android.media.AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, targetVol, 0)
                        currentVol = targetVol
                        AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                    }
                }
                .pointerInput(maxVol) {
                    detectDragGestures { change, _ ->
                        change.consume()
                        val f = (change.position.x / size.width).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
                        val targetVol = (f * maxVol).toInt().coerceIn(0, maxVol)
                        if (targetVol != currentVol) {
                            audioManager.setStreamVolume(android.media.AudioManager.STREAM_MUSIC, targetVol, 0)
                            currentVol = targetVol
                            AuroHaptics.sliderTick(view)
                        }
                    }
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(6.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f))
            )

            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth(fraction)
                    .height(6.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(Color.White)
            )
        }

        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.VolumeUp,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * AURO DOCK (Mantido para compatibilidade, encapsulando as duas barras flutuantes se necessário)
 */
@Composable
fun AuroDock(
    currentScreen: NavScreen,
    currentTrack: Track?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    progressMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    coverBitmap: Bitmap?,
    onOpenNowPlaying: () -> Unit,
    onPlayPause: () -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
    onPrevious: () -> Unit,
    onEqClick: () -> Unit,
    onLibraryClick: () -> Unit,
    onSearchClick: () -> Unit,
    onMenuClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        if (currentTrack != null) {
            AppleMiniPlayer(
                track = currentTrack,
                isPlaying = isPlaying,
                progressMs = progressMs,
                durationMs = durationMs,
                coverBitmap = coverBitmap,
                onOpenNowPlaying = onOpenNowPlaying,
                onPlayPause = onPlayPause,
                onNext = onNext,
                onPrevious = onPrevious
            )
        }

        AppleFloatingTabBar(
            currentScreen = currentScreen,
            onHomeClick = { /* Home */ },
            onLibraryClick = onLibraryClick,
            onQueueClick = { /* Queue */ },
            onSearchClick = onSearchClick
        )
    }
}

/**
 * 2. MINI PLAYER EM VIDRO PRETO (AURŌ GLASS CARD)
 * Posicionado embaixo na tela (próximo à luz global em y=0.88), captura os reflexos na borda inferior.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroMiniPlayer(
    track: Track?,
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    progressMs: Long,
    durationMs: Long,
    coverBitmap: Bitmap?,
    onOpenNowPlaying: () -> Unit,
    onPlayPause: () -> Unit,
    onNext: () -> Unit,
    onPrevious: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    if (track == null) return

    val progressFraction = if (durationMs > 0) {
        (progressMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    } else 0f

    AuroGlass(
        kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
        onClick = onOpenNowPlaying,
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(72.dp)
            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            // Linha fina de progresso de 2dp no topo do vidro
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(2.dp)
                    .background(AuroColor.Track)
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth(progressFraction)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .background(Color.White)
                )
            }

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = 12.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                val view = LocalView.current

                // Capa quadrada (40x40) com cantos arredondados
                if (coverBitmap != null) {
                    Image(
                        bitmap = coverBitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_album_cover),
                        contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(40.dp)
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                    )
                } else {
                    AuroCoverPlaceholder(
                        modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp),
                        glass = true,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                    )
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                // Título e Artista
                Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                    Text(
                        text = track.title,
                        style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = track.artist.ifEmpty { stringResource(R.string.unknown_artist) },
                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary),
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }

                // Ações de reprodução
                IconButton(onClick = { AuroHaptics.click(view); onPrevious() }, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.SkipPrevious,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_previous),
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                    )
                }

                IconButton(onClick = { AuroHaptics.confirm(view); onPlayPause() }, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = if (isPlaying) Icons.Rounded.Pause else Icons.Rounded.PlayArrow,
                        contentDescription = if (isPlaying) stringResource(R.string.cd_pause) else stringResource(R.string.cd_play),
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(26.dp)
                    )
                }

                IconButton(onClick = { AuroHaptics.click(view); onNext() }, modifier = Modifier.size(44.dp)) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.SkipNext,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_next),
                        tint = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * 3. NAVBAR INFERIOR PERSISTENTE (AURŌ NAVBAR)
 * Senta diretamente sobre o fundo preto #000000, com linha de 1dp Hairline no topo.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroNavbar(
    currentScreen: NavScreen,
    onEqClick: () -> Unit,
    onLibraryClick: () -> Unit,
    onSearchClick: () -> Unit,
    onMenuClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Surface(
        color = AuroColor.Black,
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .border(width = 1.dp, color = AuroColor.Hairline)
            .navigationBarsPadding()
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(54.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceAround,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.GraphicEq,
                label = stringResource(R.string.tab_equalizer),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Equalizer,
                onClick = onEqClick
            )

            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.LibraryMusic,
                label = stringResource(R.string.tab_library),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.LibraryRoot || currentScreen is NavScreen.CategoryList || currentScreen is NavScreen.Drilldown,
                onClick = onLibraryClick
            )

            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.Search,
                label = stringResource(R.string.tab_search),
                isActive = currentScreen is NavScreen.Search,
                onClick = onSearchClick
            )

            AuroNavbarTab(
                icon = Icons.Rounded.Menu,
                label = stringResource(R.string.tab_menu),
                isActive = false,
                onClick = onMenuClick
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AuroNavbarTab(
    icon: ImageVector,
    label: String,
    isActive: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val tint = if (isActive) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextTertiary
    val view = LocalView.current

    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isPressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.94f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.6f, stiffness = 500f),
        label = "tabScale"
    )

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .scale(scale)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                    onClick()
                }
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = icon,
            contentDescription = label,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = AuroType.NavLabel.copy(
                color = tint,
                fontWeight = if (isActive) FontWeight.Medium else FontWeight.Normal
            )
        )
    }
}

/**
 * Três barras animadas de equalizador sobre a miniatura da faixa tocando.
 * Param quando pausado ou quando reduce motion estiver ativo.
 */
@Composable
fun AuroEqualizerBars(
    isPlaying: Boolean,
    color: Color,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val reduceMotion = rememberReduceMotion()
    val shouldAnimate = isPlaying && !reduceMotion

    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "auro_eq_bars")

    val height1 by if (shouldAnimate) {
        infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
            initialValue = 0.25f,
            targetValue = 0.90f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(460, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
            ),
            label = "bar1"
        )
    } else {
        remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0.35f) }
    }

    val height2 by if (shouldAnimate) {
        infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
            initialValue = 0.45f,
            targetValue = 1.0f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(620, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
            ),
            label = "bar2"
        )
    } else {
        remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0.70f) }
    }

    val height3 by if (shouldAnimate) {
        infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
            initialValue = 0.20f,
            targetValue = 0.80f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(520, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
            ),
            label = "bar3"
        )
    } else {
        remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0.45f) }
    }

    Row(
        modifier = modifier.size(width = 18.dp, height = 18.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.5.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .width(3.dp)
                .fillMaxHeight(height1)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
                .background(color)
        )
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .width(3.dp)
                .fillMaxHeight(height2)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
                .background(color)
        )
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .width(3.dp)
                .fillMaxHeight(height3)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(1.5.dp))
                .background(color)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * 4. LINHA DE FAIXA NA LISTA (AURŌ / EDITORIAL TRACK ROW)
 * Specs: Altura 52dp, miniatura 36dp (raio 8dp), título TrackTitle (13.5sp), artista Subtitle (12sp),
 * sem divisores na lista, menu com três pontos horizontais (•••).
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun AuroTrackRow(
    track: Track,
    trackIndex: Int,
    isPlayingThis: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    onLongClick: () -> Unit,
    onMenuClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    hasError: Boolean = false,
    isSelectionMode: Boolean = false,
    isSelected: Boolean = false,
    showFormatTag: Boolean = true,
    isAudioPlaying: Boolean = LocalAuroIsPlaying.current
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isPressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val pressScale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.98f else 1f,
        animationSpec = AuroMotion.press(),
        label = "track_press_scale"
    )

    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(52.dp)
            .graphicsLayer {
                scaleX = pressScale
                scaleY = pressScale
            }
            .combinedClickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = null,
                onClick = {
                    AuroHaptics.click(view)
                    onClick()
                },
                onLongClick = {
                    if (!isSelectionMode) {
                        AuroHaptics.longPress(view)
                        onLongClick()
                    }
                }
            )
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Indicador de Seleção Múltipla: 22dp à esquerda
        if (isSelectionMode) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(22.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(if (isSelected) Color.White else Color.Transparent)
                    .border(
                        width = if (isSelected) 0.dp else 1.5.dp,
                        color = if (isSelected) Color.Transparent else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.30f),
                        shape = CircleShape
                    ),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (isSelected) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Rounded.Check,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Color.Black,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
        }

        val coverBitmap = rememberTrackCover(track, 100)

        if (coverBitmap != null) {
            // Com capa: miniatura de 36dp com raio 8dp
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(36.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(AuroRadius.thumbnail)),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Image(
                    bitmap = coverBitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                    contentDescription = null,
                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                )
                if (isPlayingThis) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxSize()
                            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.45f)),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        AuroEqualizerBars(
                            isPlaying = isAudioPlaying,
                            color = Color.White
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            // Sem capa
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                if (isPlayingThis) {
                    AuroEqualizerBars(
                        isPlaying = isAudioPlaying,
                        color = Color.White
                    )
                } else {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.MusicNote,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.45f),
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

        // Título e Artista
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                if (hasError) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.WarningAmber,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_error_indicator),
                        tint = AuroColor.Danger,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                }
                Text(
                    text = track.title,
                    style = AuroType.TrackTitle.copy(
                        color = if (hasError) AuroColor.Danger else AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                        fontWeight = if (isPlayingThis) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Medium
                    ),
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(1.dp))

            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Text(
                    text = "${track.artist} • ${track.album}",
                    style = AuroType.Subtitle,
                    color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
                )

                if (track.rating > 0) {
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = "★ ${track.rating}",
                        style = AuroType.MicroLabel.copy(
                            color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.65f),
                            fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))

        // Tag de formato de áudio (FLAC, MP3, etc.)
        if (showFormatTag && track.formatTag.isNotEmpty()) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
                    .border(0.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f), RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 1.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = track.formatTag,
                    style = AuroType.FastScroller.copy(
                        color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                )
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
        }

        // Duração da faixa
        Text(
            text = formatTimeMs(track.durationMs),
            style = AuroType.MicroLabel.copy(
                color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
            )
        )

        if (!isSelectionMode) {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(2.dp))

            // Menu da faixa: três pontos horizontais (•••)
            IconButton(
                onClick = onMenuClick,
                modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp)
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.MoreHoriz,
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_more_options),
                    tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

 * EDITORIAL TRACK ROW - Alias de AuroTrackRow
 */
@Composable
fun EditorialTrackRow(
    track: Track,
    trackIndex: Int,
    isPlayingThis: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    onLongClick: () -> Unit,
    onMenuClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,

    isSelectionMode: Boolean = false,
    isSelected: Boolean = false,
    showFormatTag: Boolean = true,

        track = track,
        trackIndex = trackIndex,
