                                    album = album,
                                    onClick = {
                                        preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                        keyboardController?.hide()
                                        onAlbumClick(album.name)
                                    }
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        SearchFilter.ARTISTS -> {
                            itemsIndexed(matchedArtists, key = { _, ar -> "artists_${ar.name}" }) { _, artist ->
                                SearchArtistRow(
                                    artist = artist,
                                    onClick = {
                                        preferencesManager.addRecentSearch(trimmed)
                                        keyboardController?.hide()
                                        onArtistClick(artist.name)
                                    }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchFilterChip(
    label: String,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = AuroHeight.TouchTarget)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null
            ) {
                AuroHaptics.tick(view)
                onClick()
            },
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .height(AuroHeight.Chip)
                .clip(shape)
                .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f) else Color.Transparent)
                .border(
                    width = 1.dp,
                    color = if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.60f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                    shape = shape
                )
                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = label,
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                    color = if (isSelected) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                )
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchSectionHeader(
    title: String,
    canViewAll: Boolean = false,
    onViewAll: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 10.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = title.uppercase(Locale.getDefault()),
            style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                letterSpacing = 1.sp
            )
        )
        if (canViewAll) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .height(24.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(AuroColor.PillMuted)
                    .clickable(onClick = onViewAll)
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.search_view_all),
                    style = AuroType.MicroLabel.copy(
                        fontSize = 11.sp,
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.75f)
                    )
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchAlbumRow(
    album: Album,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Album,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = album.name,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = "${album.artist} • ${pluralStringResource(R.plurals.tracks_count, album.trackCount, album.trackCount)}",
                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SearchArtistRow(
    artist: Artist,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Person,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
                text = artist.name,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = pluralStringResource(R.plurals.tracks_count, artist.trackCount, artist.trackCount),
                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary),
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }
        Icon(
            imageVector = Icons.Rounded.ChevronRight,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
        )
    }
}

package com.lucas.player.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.Canvas

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v2 - TELA DE EQUALIZADOR (AURŌ EQUALIZER)
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun AuroEqualizerScreen(
    eqEnabled: Boolean,
    onToggleEq: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    toneEnabled: Boolean,
    onToggleTone: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    preampDb: Float,
    onPreampChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    bassDb: Float,
    onBassChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    trebleDb: Float,
    onTrebleChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    balance: Float,
    onBalanceChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    isMono: Boolean,
    onToggleMono: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    bandGains: List<Float>,
    onBandGainChange: (Int, Float) -> Unit,
    activePresetName: String,
    presets: List<MusicDatabase.EqPreset>,
    onSelectPreset: (MusicDatabase.EqPreset) -> Unit,
    onResetEq: () -> Unit = {},
    onSavePreset: () -> Unit = {},
    onDeletePreset: (MusicDatabase.EqPreset) -> Unit = {},
    spatialMode: Int = 0,
    onSpatialModeChange: (Int) -> Unit = {},
    spatialPreset: Int = 0,
    onSpatialPresetChange: (Int) -> Unit = {},
    spatialIntensity: Float = 0.75f,
    onSpatialIntensityChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialSubCutoff: Float = 120f,
    onSpatialSubCutoffChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialMidGain: Float = 0f,
    onSpatialMidGainChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialSideWidth: Float = 1.3f,
    onSpatialSideWidthChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialSurround: Float = 0.35f,
    onSpatialSurroundChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialAngle: Float = 0.4f,
    onSpatialAngleChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialElevation: Float = 0f,
    onSpatialElevationChange: (Float) -> Unit = {},
    spatialProPreset: Int = 0,
    onSpatialProPresetChange: (Int) -> Unit = {},
    onResetSpatialPro: () -> Unit = {},
    onClose: () -> Unit,
    listState: LazyListState = rememberLazyListState(),
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val bandFreqs = remember {
        listOf("31Hz", "62Hz", "125Hz", "250Hz", "500Hz", "1kHz", "2kHz", "4kHz", "8kHz", "16kHz")
    }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Canvas)
    ) {
        AuroTopBar(
            title = stringResource(R.string.equalizer_breadcrumb),
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onClose,
            rightActions = {
                AuroSwitch(
                    checked = eqEnabled,
                    onCheckedChange = onToggleEq
                )
            }
        )

        Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            LazyColumn(
                state = listState,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 12.dp, bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
            ) {
            // Presets
            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.eq_presets_header),
                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                            color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                    )
                    AuroGlass(
                        kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                        cornerRadius = 20.dp,
                        onClick = onResetEq,
                        modifier = Modifier.height(AuroHeight.GlassButton)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp)
                                .fillMaxHeight(),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.RestartAlt,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.eq_reset),
                                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    AuroGlass(
                        kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                        cornerRadius = 20.dp,
                        onClick = onSavePreset,
                        modifier = Modifier.height(AuroHeight.GlassButton)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp)
                                .fillMaxHeight(),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Outlined.BookmarkAdd,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = Color.White,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.eq_save),
                                style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    items(presets, key = { it.id }) { p ->
                        val isSelected = p.name.equals(activePresetName, ignoreCase = true)
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .height(32.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f) else Color.Transparent)
                                .border(
                                    width = 1.dp,
                                    color = if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.60f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                                )
                                .clickable { onSelectPreset(p) }
                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = p.name,
                                style = AuroType.Body.copy(
                                    color = if (isSelected) Color.White else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Normal
                                )
                            )
                            if (!p.isBuiltin) {
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(20.dp)
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                        .clickable { onDeletePreset(p) },
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Close,
                                        contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_delete_preset),
                                        tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Áudio Espacial (HRTF + Sala FDN 4x4 + LR4)
            item {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(AuroColor.SurfaceGrouped)
                        .padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.spatial_header),
                                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                    color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                    fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                                )
                            )
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                        // 1. Seletor de 3 estados (Segmented Pill): Desativado (0) | Dolby Padrão (1) | Espacial Pro (2)
                        val view = LocalView.current
                        val modeLabels = listOf(
                            stringResource(R.string.spatial_mode_off),
                            stringResource(R.string.spatial_mode_standard),
                            stringResource(R.string.spatial_mode_pro)
                        )
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(38.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(19.dp))
                                .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.06f))
                                .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.12f), RoundedCornerShape(19.dp))
                                .padding(3.dp),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                        ) {
                            modeLabels.forEachIndexed { index, label ->
                                val isSelected = spatialMode == index
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .fillMaxHeight()
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                        .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f) else Color.Transparent)
                                        .border(
                                            width = if (isSelected) 1.dp else 0.dp,
                                            color = if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.60f) else Color.Transparent,
                                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                                        )
                                        .clickable {
                                            AuroHaptics.tick(view)
                                            onSpatialModeChange(index)
                                        },
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = label,
                                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                            color = if (isSelected) Color.White else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium
                                        ),
                                        maxLines = 1,
                                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Modo 0: Desativado
                        if (spatialMode == 0) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(R.string.spatial_off_desc),
                                style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextTertiary)
                            )
                        }

                        // Modo 1: Dolby Padrão
                        if (spatialMode == 1) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))
                            // 3 Chips selecionáveis: Música (0), Cinema (1), Palco (2)
                            val presetLabels = listOf(
                                stringResource(R.string.spatial_preset_music),
                                stringResource(R.string.spatial_preset_cinema),
                                stringResource(R.string.spatial_preset_stage)
                            )
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                presetLabels.forEachIndexed { index, label ->
                                    val isSelected = spatialPreset == index
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .weight(1f)
                                            .height(34.dp)
                                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(17.dp))
                                            .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f) else Color.Transparent)
                                            .border(
                                                width = 1.dp,
                                                color = if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.60f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(17.dp)
                                            )
                                            .clickable {
                                                AuroHaptics.tick(view)
                                                onSpatialPresetChange(index)
                                            }
                                            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = label,
                                            style = AuroType.Body.copy(
                                                color = if (isSelected) Color.White else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                                fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                                fontSize = 13.sp
                                            ),
                                            maxLines = 1,
                                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }

                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                            // 1 slider de Intensidade (0% a 100%)
                            val formattedIntensity = "${(spatialIntensity * 100).roundToInt()}%"
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_intensity_label),
                                value = spatialIntensity,
                                range = 0f..1f,
                                formattedValue = formattedIntensity,
                                onValueChange = onSpatialIntensityChange
                            )
                        }

                        // Modo 2: Espacial Pro
                        if (spatialMode == 2) {
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                            // 5 Presets Waves Nx + Personalizado
                            val proPresetLabels = listOf(
                                "Estúdio Nx",
                                "Abbey Road",
                                "Cinema 360°",
                                "Arena 3D",
                                "Personalizado"
                            )
                            LazyRow(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                items(proPresetLabels.size) { index ->
                                    val label = proPresetLabels[index]
                                    val isSelected = spatialProPreset == index
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .height(34.dp)
                                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(17.dp))
                                            .background(if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f) else Color.Transparent)
                                            .border(
                                                width = 1.dp,
                                                color = if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.60f) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f),
                                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(17.dp)
                                            )
                                            .clickable {
                                                AuroHaptics.tick(view)
                                                onSpatialProPresetChange(index)
                                            }
                                            .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = label,
                                            style = AuroType.Body.copy(
                                                color = if (isSelected) Color.White else AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                                fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                                fontSize = 13.sp
                                            ),
                                            maxLines = 1,
                                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }

                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "PARÂMETROS ACÚSTICOS 3D",
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                        fontSize = 11.sp
                                    )
                                )
                                AuroGlass(
                                    kind = AuroGlassKind.Card,
                                    cornerRadius = 16.dp,
                                    onClick = onResetSpatialPro,
                                    modifier = Modifier.height(30.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp)
                                            .fillMaxHeight(),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            imageVector = Icons.Outlined.RestartAlt,
                                            contentDescription = null,
                                            tint = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                        )
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                        Text(
                                            text = stringResource(R.string.spatial_reset_pro),
                                            style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                                                color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                                fontSize = 11.sp
                                            )
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }

                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                            // 1. Corte Sub-Grave: 60 Hz – 250 Hz (controla o crossover LR4)
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_sub_cutoff_label),
                                value = spatialSubCutoff,
                                range = 60f..250f,
                                formattedValue = "${spatialSubCutoff.roundToInt()} Hz",
                                onValueChange = onSpatialSubCutoffChange
                            )

                            // 2. Foco Vocal (Mid): -6.0 dB – +6.0 dB (projeção da caixa central)
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_mid_gain_label),
                                value = spatialMidGain,
                                range = -6f..6f,
                                formattedValue = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%+.1f dB", spatialMidGain),
                                onValueChange = onSpatialMidGainChange
                            )

                            // 3. Largura de Palco: 50% – 220% (abertura L/R)
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_side_width_label),
                                value = spatialSideWidth,
                                range = 0.5f..2.2f,
                                formattedValue = "${(spatialSideWidth * 100).roundToInt()}%",
                                onValueChange = onSpatialSideWidthChange
                            )

                            // 4. Acústica da Sala: 0% – 100% (Early Reflections + Matriz FDN Hadamard 4x4)
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_surround_label),
                                value = spatialSurround,
                                range = 0f..1f,
                                formattedValue = "${(spatialSurround * 100).roundToInt()}%",
                                onValueChange = onSpatialSurroundChange
                            )

                            // 5. Ângulo Binaural: 15° – 65° (Steam Audio HRTF)
                            val degrees = (15f + spatialAngle * 50f).roundToInt()
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_angle_label),
                                value = spatialAngle,
                                range = 0f..1f,
                                formattedValue = "$degrees°",
                                onValueChange = onSpatialAngleChange
                            )

                            // 6. Altura 3D (Elevação): -35° – +35° (Steam Audio HRTF)
                            val elevDegrees = (spatialElevation * 35).toInt()
                            AuroSpatialSliderRow(
                                label = stringResource(R.string.spatial_elevation_label),
                                value = spatialElevation,
                                range = -1f..1f,
                                formattedValue = "$elevDegrees°",
                                onValueChange = onSpatialElevationChange
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

            // Tom & Pré-amp
            item {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(AuroColor.SurfaceGrouped)
                        .padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.eq_tone_preamp_header),
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                                    )
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                                AuroSwitch(
                                    checked = toneEnabled,
                                    onCheckedChange = onToggleTone,
                                    enabled = eqEnabled
                                )
                            }
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.eq_mono),
                                    style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Checkbox(
                                    checked = isMono,
                                    onCheckedChange = onToggleMono,
                                    colors = CheckboxDefaults.colors(checkedColor = Color.White, checkmarkColor = Color.Black)
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_preamp_label), value = preampDb, range = -12f..12f, enabled = eqEnabled, onValueChange = onPreampChange)
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_bass_label), value = bassDb, range = -12f..12f, enabled = eqEnabled && toneEnabled, onValueChange = onBassChange)
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_treble_label), value = trebleDb, range = -12f..12f, enabled = eqEnabled && toneEnabled, onValueChange = onTrebleChange)
                        AuroToneRow(label = stringResource(R.string.eq_balance_label), value = balance, range = -1f..1f, isBalance = true, enabled = true, onValueChange = onBalanceChange)
                    }
                }

            // 10 Bandas
            item {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                        .background(AuroColor.SurfaceGrouped)
                        .padding(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.eq_bands_header),
                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                            color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                            fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                        )
                    )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                        // Curva de resposta gráfica de 2dp interpolada das 10 bandas
                        Canvas(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(48.dp)
                                .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                        ) {
                            val w = size.width
                            val h = size.height
                            val midY = h / 2f

                            // Linha de referência central 0 dB
                            drawLine(
                                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.10f),
                                start = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset(0f, midY),
                                end = androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset(w, midY),
                                strokeWidth = 1.dp.toPx()
                            )

                            if (bandGains.isNotEmpty()) {
                                val path = Path()
                                val stepX = if (bandGains.size > 1) w / (bandGains.size - 1) else w

                                val points = bandGains.mapIndexed { index, gain ->
                                    val clamped = gain.coerceIn(-12f, 12f)
                                    val x = index * stepX
                                    val y = midY - (clamped / 12f) * (h * 0.40f)
                                    androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset(x, y)
                                }

                                path.moveTo(points.first().x, points.first().y)
                                for (i in 0 until points.size - 1) {
                                    val p0 = points[i]
                                    val p1 = points[i + 1]
                                    val cx = (p0.x + p1.x) / 2f
                                    path.cubicTo(cx, p0.y, cx, p1.y, p1.x, p1.y)
                                }

                                drawPath(
                                    path = path,
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    style = Stroke(
                                        width = 2.dp.toPx(),
                                        cap = StrokeCap.Round,
                                        join = StrokeJoin.Round
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                        bandFreqs.forEachIndexed { bandIndex, freqLabel ->
                            val gain = bandGains.getOrElse(bandIndex) { 0f }
                            val formattedGain = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%+.1f dB", gain)
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(vertical = 2.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = freqLabel,
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
                                    ),
                                    modifier = Modifier.width(50.dp)
                                )

                                AuroSlider(
                                    value = gain,
                                    onValueChange = { onBandGainChange(bandIndex, it) },
                                    valueRange = -12f..12f,
                                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.cd_eq_band, freqLabel),
                                    stateDescription = formattedGain,
                                    enabled = eqEnabled,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(36.dp)
                                )

                                Text(
                                    text = formattedGain,
                                    style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                                        color = AuroColor.TextSecondary,
                                        fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                                        fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
                                    ),
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.End,
                                    modifier = Modifier.width(54.dp)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

        // Fade sutil de 16dp no topo
        AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
    }
}
}

@Composable
private fun AuroToneRow(
    label: String,
    value: Float,
    range: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float>,
    isBalance: Boolean = false,
    enabled: Boolean = true,
    onValueChange: (Float) -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(vertical = 2.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                color = if (enabled) AuroColor.TextSecondary else AuroColor.TextTertiary
            ),
            modifier = Modifier.width(86.dp)
        )
        val textVal = if (isBalance) {
            when {
                value < -0.05f -> "L ${(value * -100).roundToInt()}%"
                value > 0.05f -> "R ${(value * 100).roundToInt()}%"
                else -> stringResource(R.string.eq_balance_center)
            }
        } else {
            String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%+.1f dB", value)
        }
        AuroSlider(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            valueRange = range,
            contentDescription = label,
            stateDescription = textVal,
            enabled = enabled,
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .height(36.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = textVal,
            style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                color = if (enabled) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
            ),
            textAlign = TextAlign.End,
            modifier = Modifier.width(54.dp)
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AuroSpatialSliderRow(
    label: String,
    value: Float,
    range: ClosedFloatingPointRange<Float>,
    formattedValue: String,
    onValueChange: (Float) -> Unit,
    enabled: Boolean = true
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(vertical = 2.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                color = if (enabled) AuroColor.TextSecondary else AuroColor.TextTertiary
            ),
            modifier = Modifier.width(136.dp),
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
        )
        AuroSlider(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            valueRange = range,
            contentDescription = label,
            stateDescription = formattedValue,
            enabled = enabled,
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .height(36.dp)
        )
        Text(
            text = formattedValue,
            style = AuroType.Caption.copy(
                color = if (enabled) AuroColor.TextPrimary else AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
                fontFeatureSettings = "tnum"
            ),
            textAlign = TextAlign.End,
            modifier = Modifier.width(64.dp)
        )
    }
}

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v3 - TELA DE CONFIGURAÇÕES (AURŌ SETTINGS SCREEN)
 * Fase 4.2: Configurações persistidas no PreferencesManager, alinhadas às diretrizes do plano:
 * - Cabeçalhos em Label maiúsculo com Accent A
 * - Linhas mínimas de 56dp com divisor Hairline
 * - AuroSwitch (48x28dp) e AuroSlider com semantics
 * - Seções: Biblioteca, Reprodução, Aparência (com botão de vidro de exemplo ao vivo) e Sobre
 */
@Composable
fun AuroSettingsScreen(
    preferencesManager: PreferencesManager,
    onBackClick: () -> Unit,
    onRescanLibrary: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onSettingsChanged: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val accent = LocalAuroAccent.current.a.value

    // Estados Reativos de Preferências
    var pauseOnDisconnect by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isPauseOnHeadsetDisconnectEnabled()) }
    var gapless by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isGaplessEnabled()) }
    var crossfadeSeconds by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getCrossfadeDurationMs() / 1000) }
    var replayGainMode by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getReplayGainMode()) }
    var rgPreampWith by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithRg()) }
    var rgPreampWithout by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(preferencesManager.getReplayGainPreampWithoutRg()) }
    var resumePlayback by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isResumePlaybackEnabled()) }

    var tiltEnabled by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isLightReactionTiltEnabled()) }
    var reflectionSoftness by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(preferencesManager.getReflectionSoftness()) }
    var reduceMotionMode by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getReduceMotionMode()) }
    var showFileFormat by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isShowFileFormatEnabled()) }
    var reduceTransparency by remember { mutableStateOf(preferencesManager.isReduceTransparencyEnabled()) }

    var ignoreShorterThanSec by remember { mutableIntStateOf(preferencesManager.getIgnoreShorterThanSeconds()) }

    // Diálogos de Seleção
    var showReplayGainPicker by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showReduceMotionPicker by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showLicensesDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(AuroColor.Canvas)
    ) {
        AuroTopBar(
            title = stringResource(R.string.settings_title),
            canGoBack = true,
            onBackClick = onBackClick
        )

        Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalAuroBottomInset.current + 24.dp)
            ) {
            // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            // SEÇÃO 1: BIBLIOTECA
            // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            item {
                SettingsSectionHeader(stringResource(R.string.settings_section_library))
                SettingsCard {
                    SettingsClickableRow(
                        title = stringResource(R.string.settings_music_folders),
                        subtitle = stringResource(R.string.settings_music_folders_desc),
                        onClick = { onRescanLibrary() },
                        trailingContent = {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Rounded.FolderOpen,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    )
                    HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 0.5.dp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp))
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp)
                    ) {

                                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)

                                },
                                style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,

                                preferencesManager.setIgnoreShorterThanSeconds(rounded)

                            steps = 11
                        )
                    }
                    HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 0.5.dp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp))
                    SettingsClickableRow(

                    HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 0.5.dp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp))
                    Column(

                        title = stringResource(R.string.settings_licenses),

                        onClick = { showLicensesDialog = true }
                    )
                    HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 0.5.dp, modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp))
                    SettingsClickableRow(
                        title = stringResource(R.string.settings_repository),
                        subtitle = "GitHub • Aurō Audio Engine v2.0",
                        onClick = {
                            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://github.com/Lusqta/Player"))
                            try {
                                context.startActivity(intent)

                        },

        }

        // Fade sutil de 16dp no topo
        AuroTopFade(modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.TopCenter))
    }
}

    // Modal Sheet de Escolha do ReplayGain
    if (showReplayGainPicker) {
        AuroOverlay(
            visible = true,
            onDismiss = { showReplayGainPicker = false },
            type = AuroOverlayType.BottomSheet
        ) {
            AuroSheetContainer(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain),
                onDismiss = { showReplayGainPicker = false }
            ) {
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (replayGainMode == 0) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_off),
                    onClick = {
                        replayGainMode = 0
                        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(0)
                        showReplayGainPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (replayGainMode == 1) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_track),
                    onClick = {
                        replayGainMode = 1
                        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(1)
                        showReplayGainPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (replayGainMode == 2) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_replaygain_album),
                    onClick = {
                        replayGainMode = 2
                        preferencesManager.saveReplayGainMode(2)
                        showReplayGainPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // Modal Sheet de Escolha de Animações
    if (showReduceMotionPicker) {
        AuroOverlay(
            visible = true,
            onDismiss = { showReduceMotionPicker = false },
            type = AuroOverlayType.BottomSheet
        ) {
            AuroSheetContainer(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion),
                onDismiss = { showReduceMotionPicker = false }
            ) {
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (reduceMotionMode == 0) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion_system),
                    onClick = {
                        reduceMotionMode = 0
                        preferencesManager.setReduceMotionMode(0)
                        showReduceMotionPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (reduceMotionMode == 1) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion_always),
                    onClick = {
                        reduceMotionMode = 1
                        preferencesManager.setReduceMotionMode(1)
                        showReduceMotionPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
                AuroDialogOption(
                    icon = if (reduceMotionMode == 2) Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonChecked else Icons.Rounded.RadioButtonUnchecked,
                    title = stringResource(R.string.settings_reduce_motion_never),
                    onClick = {
                        reduceMotionMode = 2
                        preferencesManager.setReduceMotionMode(2)
                        showReduceMotionPicker = false
                        onSettingsChanged()
                    }
                )
            }
        }
    }

    // Diálogo de Licenças
    if (showLicensesDialog) {
        AuroOverlay(
            visible = true,
            onDismiss = { showLicensesDialog = false },
            type = AuroOverlayType.Dialog
        ) {
            AuroDialogContainer(
                title = stringResource(R.string.settings_licenses),
                onDismiss = { showLicensesDialog = false },
                actions = {
                    AuroDialogTextButton(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.close),
                        onClick = { showLicensesDialog = false }
                    )
                }
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.settings_licenses_text),
                    style = AuroType.Body.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsCard(
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
            .background(AuroColor.SurfaceCard),
        content = content
    )
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsSectionHeader(title: String) {
    Text(
        text = title,
        style = AuroType.SectionHeader.copy(
            color = AuroColor.TextTertiary,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace
        ),
        modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 28.dp, top = 20.dp, bottom = 8.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsSwitchRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = 56.dp)
            .clickable { onCheckedChange(!checked) }
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 16.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
            )
        }
        AuroSwitch(
            checked = checked,
            onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SettingsClickableRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    trailingContent: (@Composable () -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val clickMod = if (onClick != null) Modifier.clickable(onClick = onClick) else Modifier

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = 56.dp)
            .then(clickMod)
            .padding(horizontal = AuroSpace.ScreenMargin, vertical = 12.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).padding(end = 12.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = AuroType.Body.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
            )
        }
        trailingContent?.invoke()
    }
}

/**
 * AURŌ DESIGN SYSTEM v3 - SOBREPOSIÇÕES (FASE 7.5)
 * - Scrim preto a 72% via AuroOverlay.
 * - AuroDialogContainer: card único de AuroGlass(Card) raio 28, largura da tela menos 56dp (máx. 360), padding 24.
 *   Título Subtitle; opções de 48dp (ícone outlined 20dp e Body); ações como botões de texto Label em negrito,
 *   accent A, alinhados à direita, altura mínima 48dp; ação destrutiva em AuroColor.Danger.
 * - AuroSheetContainer: cantos superiores de 28dp, alça de 36x4dp em branco a 24%, itens de 56dp, altura máxima 80% da tela.
 * - Um único vidro por overlay (sem vidro dentro de vidro).
 * - Campos de texto com BasicTextField e borda fina 1dp (0x2EFFFFFF).
 */

@Composable
fun AuroDialogContainer(
    title: String,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    actions: (@Composable RowScope.() -> Unit)? = null,
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val screenWidth = LocalConfiguration.current.screenWidthDp.dp
    val dialogWidth = (screenWidth - 56.dp).coerceAtMost(360.dp)

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .width(dialogWidth)
            .padding(vertical = 16.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
            .background(AuroColor.SurfaceGrouped)
            .border(0.5.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.08f), RoundedCornerShape(28.dp))
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(24.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = AuroType.Subtitle.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
            if (!subtitle.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = subtitle,
                    style = AuroType.Label.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary),
                    maxLines = 1,
                    overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                )
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

            content()

            if (actions != null) {
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    content = actions
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun AuroDialogTextButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    isDestructive: Boolean = false
) {
    val view = LocalView.current
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = AuroHeight.TouchTarget)
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null
            ) {
                AuroHaptics.click(view)
                onClick()
            }
            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Text(
            text = text,
            style = AuroType.Label.copy(
                color = if (isDestructive) AuroColor.Danger else Color.White,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun AuroSheetContainer(
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    title: String? = null,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit
) {
    val screenHeight = LocalConfiguration.current.screenHeightDp.dp
    val maxHeight = screenHeight * 0.80f

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .heightIn(max = maxHeight)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(topStart = 28.dp, topEnd = 28.dp))
            .background(AuroColor.SurfaceGrouped)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .navigationBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            // Alça de 36x4dp em branco a 24%
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(width = 36.dp, height = 4.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(2.dp))
                    .background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.24f))
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

            if (!title.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                Text(
                    text = title,
                    style = AuroType.Subtitle.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                    color = AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )
                if (!subtitle.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                    Text(
                        text = subtitle,
                        style = AuroType.Caption.copy(color = AuroColor.TextTertiary),
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    )
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
            }

            content()
        }
    }
}

// 1. MENU CONTEXTUAL DA FAIXA (AURŌ TRACK CONTEXT MENU)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@Composable
fun AuroTrackContextMenuDialog(
    track: Track,
    onPlayNext: () -> Unit,
    onEnqueueEnd: () -> Unit,
    onAddToPlaylist: () -> Unit,
    onSetRating: (Int) -> Unit,
    onShowInfo: () -> Unit,
    onStartSelection: () -> Unit = {},
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    AuroOverlay(
        visible = true,
        onDismiss = onDismiss,
        type = AuroOverlayType.Dialog
    ) {
        AuroDialogContainer(
            title = track.title,
            subtitle = track.artist.ifEmpty { stringResource(R.string.unknown_artist) },
            onDismiss = onDismiss,
            actions = {
                AuroDialogTextButton(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.close),
                    onClick = onDismiss
                )
            }
        ) {
            // Classificação por estrelas
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.context_rating),
                    style = AuroType.Body.copy(color = AuroColor.TextSecondary)
                )
                AuroRatingBar(
                    rating = track.rating,
                    onRatingSelected = onSetRating
                )
            }

            HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp, modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 8.dp))

            AuroDialogOption(
                icon = Icons.Outlined.Checklist,
                title = stringResource(R.string.selection_select),
                onClick = {
                    onDismiss()
                    onStartSelection()
                }
            )
            AuroDialogOption(
                icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.QueueMusic,
                title = stringResource(R.string.context_play_next),
                onClick = onPlayNext
            )
            AuroDialogOption(
                icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.PlaylistPlay,
                title = stringResource(R.string.context_add_queue),
                onClick = onEnqueueEnd
            )
            AuroDialogOption(
                icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.PlaylistAdd,
                title = stringResource(R.string.context_add_playlist),
                onClick = onAddToPlaylist
            )
            AuroDialogOption(
                icon = Icons.Outlined.Info,
                title = stringResource(R.string.context_track_info),
                onClick = onShowInfo
            )
        }
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. DIÁLOGO DE ORDENAÇÃO (AURŌ SORT DIALOG)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@Composable
fun AuroSortDialog(
    currentSortMode: SortMode,
    onSelectSort: (SortMode) -> Unit,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    AuroOverlay(
        visible = true,
        onDismiss = onDismiss,
        type = AuroOverlayType.Dialog
    ) {
        AuroDialogContainer(
            title = stringResource(R.string.sort_title),
            onDismiss = onDismiss,
            actions = {
                AuroDialogTextButton(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.close),
                    onClick = onDismiss
                )
            }
        ) {
            LazyColumn(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(max = 280.dp)) {
                items(SortMode.entries.toTypedArray()) { mode ->
                    val isSelected = mode == currentSortMode
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(AuroHeight.DialogOption)
                            .clickable {
                                onSelectSort(mode)
                                onDismiss()
                            }
                            .padding(horizontal = 4.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isSelected,
                            onClick = null,
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(
                                selectedColor = Color.White,
                                unselectedColor = AuroColor.TextTertiary
                            )
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(mode.getDisplayNameRes()),
                            style = AuroType.Body.copy(
                                color = if (isSelected) Color.White else AuroColor.TextPrimary,
                                fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    HorizontalDivider(color = AuroColor.Hairline, thickness = 1.dp)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
